📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期猴痘病毒(Mpox病毒)研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 分支簇I 猴痘 的汇总再生数(R)从1970-2017年的0.71(95% CI 0.26至1.17)上升至2023年后亚分支簇Ib/Ia的1.23(1.12-1.33)。分支簇II 猴痘 在2017-2021年间的R为1.11(0.90-1.32),而在2022-2023年亚分支簇IIb则升至2.66(2.31-3.00)。实证模型研究估计,行为改变与疫苗接种 共同使2022年亚分支簇IIb 暴发 期间的猴痘 病例减少了55%(95% CI 37至73),但效应规模在不同环境中存在差异。
  • 2024年共报告2,803例猴痘病毒感染病例,其中包括1例与旅行相关的Ib分支病例;2025年共报告2,519例病例,其中包括10例Ib分支病例。2025年9–10月报告的病例数是2024年同期的两倍,但截至2025年12月底,病例数已回落至背景水平。在860名信息完整的患者中,未接种疫苗者的住院风险是完全接种疫苗者的9.73倍(在控制年龄组和HIV感染状况后)。
  • 该检测方法在线性度、分析灵敏度(检测限在各靶标间为6.7至9.6拷贝/反应)以及在分支水平区分和定量猴痘病毒的特异性方面表现优异。 [10.1099/acmi.0.001041.v3]。
  • 整体‘一体化健康’对齐程度有限,无任何疾病×文件类型×目标组合的对齐率超过39%。文件类型差异最为显著:在协调与暴发应对方面,机构技术指导文件的对齐率分别比《联邦公报》文件高13至30个百分点(p值均<0.001)。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • The pooled reproduction number (R) for clade I mpox increased from 0.71 (95% CI 0.26 to 1.17) during 1970-2017 to 1.23 (1.12-1.33) for subclades Ib/Ia after 2023.
  • The pooled prevalence of mpox among MSM was estimated at 2.30% [95% confidence interval (CI): 1.29–3.57%]. [10.1186/s40249-026-01493-y].
  • In 2024, 2,803 MPXV cases were reported, including one travel-associated clade Ib case, while in 2025, 2,519 cases were reported, including 10 clade Ib cases.
  • The assay demonstrated strong linearity, analytical sensitivity (limits of detection ranged from 6.7 to 9.6 copies per reaction across targets) and specificity in distinguishing and quantifying MPXV by clade.

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 本期猴痘病毒(Mpox病毒)新闻集中呈现以下动态。
  • CDC提供了猴痘病毒的临床概述,特别强调了I型和II型猴痘病毒分支的差异。文件指出,两种分支的传播、治疗和预防方法相似,但其病死率和传播模式可能存在差异。该文件还为医护人员提供了临床检测、诊断和预防的指导。
  • 美国疾病控制与预防中心(CDC)报告称,基于对三名在感染状态下乘机进入或在美国境内飞行的感染者的调查,I支系猴痘病毒(MPXV)在商业航空旅行期间的传播风险极低。 [news-9d171ac0fae55320]。
  • CDC研究显示猴痘可能正在美国成为常驻病原体。该结论为CDC基于研究结果的确认性陈述。 [news-d9b3c522df58348c]。
  • UNICEF报告称,几内亚比绍46例疑似猴痘病例中有23例为15岁以下儿童,表明在最年幼且最脆弱的人群中可能存在隐性传播传播。该暴发是该国首次记录的猴痘暴发。 [news-c9e44fd7cc6feaf4]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following Mpox virus news developments.
  • The CDC provides a clinical overview of the monkeypox virus, specifically highlighting the differences between clade I and clade II MPXV.
  • The CDC reported that the transmission risk for clade I monkeypox virus (MPXV) during commercial air travel appears to be very low, based on investigations involving three infected travelers who flew into or within the United States while infectious.
  • The CDC study suggests that mpox is likely becoming endemic in the United States. This is a reported statement by the CDC, and the status is confirmed based on the study's findings. [news-d9b3c522df58348c].
  • UNICEF reported that 23 of Guinea-Bissau's 46 suspected mpox cases are children under 15, indicating a potential silent transmission among the youngest and most vulnerable population.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Reviews in medical virology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-09-01  |  DOI: 10.1002/rmv.70196  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Future scheduled publication  |  Citation: 2026, 36, 5, e70196

猴痘 机载 传播:系统回顾。

Mpox Airborne Transmission: A Systematic Review.
作者： Lucas Ferreira Theotonio Dos Santos, Leonardo Javier Arcuri, Viviane Aparecida R Sant'Anna, Patrícia Campos Couto, Guilherme de Paula P Schettino, Luiz Vicente Rizzo, Henrique Andrade R Fonseca
摘要中文翻译自 2022 年 5 月以来,多个非流行地区均报告了 猴痘,并在不同的地理环境中同时发生了 暴发,引发了人们对更广泛的国际传播的担忧。然而,有关空气传播 传播 风险的证据仍然有限。我们进行了系统审查,以评估空气中 传播 的风险以及空气样本中是否存在活病毒。检索了 PubMed、Web of Science 和 Cochrane 图书馆以英文发表的研究,包括环境研究、病例报告、横断面研究和 队列研究 研究。数据提取包括研究特征(作者、年份、设计和背景)、样本量、诊断方法、参与者特征、随访、评估的呼吸道颗粒类型(飞沫和/或气溶胶)以及主要发现。在确定的 286 份记录中,有四项研究符合纳入标准。所有这些都是 2022 年至 2023 年间发表的环境研究。在表面和空气样本中检测到 MPXV DNA,循环阈值 (Ct) 值范围为 < 30 到 < 45。然而,没有一项研究表明气溶胶或飞沫中病毒的生存能力一致,足以支持空气中的 传播。总体而言,现有证据有限,并不支持空气传播感染的明确风险。需要在人类环境中进行进一步精心设计的研究,以阐明 猴痘 传播 通过呼吸途径的潜力。系统审查注册:PROSPERO:CRD42023391123。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估 猴痘 空气传播的风险以及空气样本中是否存在活病毒,因为 猴痘 已在多个非流行地区报告,并担心国际传播。
设计与对象
对猴痘的空气传播传播的风险进行了系统评价,包括2022年至2023年期间发表的环境研究、病例报告、横断面研究和队列研究研究。
核心方法
该综述涉及在PubMed、Web of Science和Cochrane Library中检索英文发表的研究,数据提取重点关注研究特征、样本量、诊断方法、参与者详情、呼吸颗粒类型及主要发现。
主要结果
在表面和空气样本中检测到 MPXV DNA,循环阈值 (Ct) 值范围为 < 30 至 < 45。然而,没有一项研究表明气溶胶或液滴中病毒的生存能力一致,足以支持空气中的 传播。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,现有证据并不支持空气传播感染的明显风险,强调需要在人类环境中进行进一步精心设计的研究,以阐明 猴痘 传播 通过呼吸道途径的可能性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,目前的证据不足以证实猴痘在空气中传播,这对公共卫生措施以及制定有针对性的监测和预防策略具有影响。
局限与证据强度
证据有限且基于环境研究,可能无法充分代表人类传播动态,导致中等置信度评估。

Mpox Airborne Transmission: A Systematic Review.

Authors: Lucas Ferreira Theotonio Dos Santos, Leonardo Javier Arcuri, Viviane Aparecida R Sant'Anna, Patrícia Campos Couto, Guilherme de Paula P Schettino, Luiz Vicente Rizzo, Henrique Andrade R Fonseca
Original abstract (English)
Since May 2022, mpox has been reported across multiple nonendemic regions, with concurrent outbreaks in diverse geographic settings, raising concerns about broader international spread. However, evidence regarding the risk of airborne transmission remains limited. We conducted a systematic review to evaluate the risk of airborne transmission and the presence of viable viruses in air samples. PubMed, Web of Science, and the Cochrane Library were searched for studies published in English, including environmental studies, case reports, cross-sectional studies, and cohort studies. Data extraction included study characteristics (authors, year, design, and setting), sample size, diagnostic methods, participant characteristics, follow-up, type of respiratory particles assessed (droplets and/or aerosols), and key findings. Of 286 records identified, four studies met the inclusion criteria. All were environmental studies published between 2022 and 2023. MPXV DNA was detected in surface and air samples, with cycle threshold (Ct) values ranging from < 30 to < 45. However, none of the studies demonstrated consistent viral viability in aerosols or droplets sufficient to support airborne transmission. Overall, the available evidence is limited and does not support a clear risk of airborne infection. Further well-designed studies in human settings are needed to clarify the potential for mpox transmission through respiratory routes. SYSTEMATIC REVIEW REGISTRATION: PROSPERO: CRD42023391123.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the risk of airborne transmission of mpox and the presence of viable viruses in air samples, as mpox has been reported across multiple nonendemic regions with concerns about international spread.
Design and population
A systematic review was conducted to assess the risk of airborne transmission of mpox, including environmental studies, case reports, cross-sectional studies, and cohort studies published between 2022 and 2023.
Core methods
The review involved searching PubMed, Web of Science, and the Cochrane Library for studies published in English, with data extraction focusing on study characteristics, sample size, diagnostic methods, participant details, respiratory particle types, and key findings.
Main results
MPXV DNA was detected in surface and air samples, with cycle threshold (Ct) values ranging from < 30 to < 45. However, none of the studies demonstrated consistent viral viability in aerosols or droplets sufficient to support airborne transmission.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that the available evidence does not support a clear risk of airborne infection, highlighting the need for further well-designed studies in human settings to clarify the potential for mpox transmission through respiratory routes.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that current evidence is insufficient to confirm airborne transmission of mpox, which has implications for public health measures and the development of targeted surveillance and prevention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited and based on environmental studies, which may not fully represent human transmission dynamics, resulting in a moderate confidence assessment.

猴痘病毒 监测 — 美国,2024–2025

Monkeypox Virus Surveillance — United States, 2024–2025
作者： Ryan Sandford, Alexandra Tuttle, Leah Aeschleman, Crystal M. Gigante, Whitni Davidson, Kimberly Wilkins, Panayampalli S. Satheshkumar, Agha Ajmal, Christina L. Hutson, Faisal S. Minhaj
摘要中文翻译自2022年猴痘病毒(MPXV)感染在全球暴发以来,美国的病例数已显著下降;然而,低水平传播仍持续存在。为评估2024–2025年美国猴痘病毒感染病例的趋势,研究人员分析了来自“One CDC数据平台”和“国家法定报告疾病监测系统”的数据。2024年,美国共报告2,803例猴痘病毒感染病例,其中包括1例与旅行相关的Ib分支病例。2025年,共报告2,519例病例,其中包括10例Ib分支病例。2025年9–10月报告的病例数是2024年同期的两倍,但截至2025年12月底,病例数已回落至背景水平。猴痘病毒感染患者的人口学特征(包括性别、种族和民族、性取向、病变部位及年龄分布)与以往报告相似。在860名信息完整的患者中,未接种疫苗者的住院风险是完全接种疫苗者的9.73倍(在控制年龄组和HIV感染状况后)。鼓励完成针对猴痘病毒暴露风险人群(包括赴正在流行I或II分支猴痘病毒的特定国家的旅行者)推荐的2剂疫苗接种,可降低重症和住院风险。持续常规监测对于限制未来传播、监测病毒进化变化并将疫苗接种工作聚焦于受影响地区仍具有重要意义。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在在2022年全球猴痘病毒暴发后,尽管美国病例数大幅下降,但仍需表征该国猴痘病毒的持续低水平传播现状。
设计与对象
研究人员分析了2024–2025年美国报告的猴痘病毒感染病例数据,数据来源于“One CDC数据平台”和“国家法定报告疾病监测系统”。
核心方法
分析包括病例数量、人口学分布、分支分型以及评估疫苗接种状况与住院结局的关联(使用调整后的优势比)。
主要结果
2024年共报告2,803例猴痘病毒感染病例,其中包括1例与旅行相关的Ib分支病例;2025年共报告2,519例病例,其中包括10例Ib分支病例。2025年9–10月报告的病例数是2024年同期的两倍,但截至2025年12月底,病例数已回落至背景水平。在860名信息完整的患者中,未接种疫苗者的住院风险是完全接种疫苗者的9.73倍(在控制年龄组和HIV感染状况后)。
解释与创新
作者将Ib分支病例的再次出现以及2025年末病例数的倍增解释为持续传播的证据,表明需要持续监测和针对性疫苗接种。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持优先完成针对猴痘病毒暴露风险人群(包括赴正在流行I或II分支猴痘病毒的特定国家的旅行者)推荐的2剂疫苗接种,以减少重症和住院风险。
局限与证据强度
本研究依赖被动监测数据,可能低估病例数量,且缺乏所有报告病例的详细临床或基因组数据,限制了因果推断和结果的普遍性。

Monkeypox Virus Surveillance — United States, 2024–2025

Authors: Ryan Sandford, Alexandra Tuttle, Leah Aeschleman, Crystal M. Gigante, Whitni Davidson, Kimberly Wilkins, Panayampalli S. Satheshkumar, Agha Ajmal, Christina L. Hutson, Faisal S. Minhaj
Original abstract (English)
Since the onset of a global outbreak of monkeypox virus (MPXV) infections in 2022, cases have declined substantially in the United States; however, ongoing low-level transmission has persisted. To assess trends in cases of MPXV infection in the United States during 2024-2025, investigators analyzed data from the One CDC Data Platform and the National Notifiable Diseases Surveillance System. In 2024, a total of 2,803 cases of MPXV infection were reported in the United States, including one travel-associated clade Ib case. In 2025, a total of 2,519 cases were reported, including 10 clade Ib cases. The number of reported cases of MPXV infection during September-October 2025 was double that during the same period in 2024, although cases returned to background levels by the end of December 2025. Demographic characteristics of patients with MPXV infections, including distributions by sex, race and ethnicity, sexual orientation, lesion location, and age, were similar to those reported in previous years. Among 860 patients with complete information, the odds of hospitalization among those who were unvaccinated were 9.73 times those who were fully vaccinated when controlling for age group and HIV status. Encouraging completion of the recommended 2-dose vaccination series for persons at risk for MPXV exposure, including travelers to specific countries with ongoing clade I or clade II transmission, could reduce the risk for severe illness and hospitalization. Continued routine surveillance remains important to limiting future spread, monitoring changes in viral evolution, and focusing vaccination efforts on affected areas.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the need to characterize ongoing low-level transmission of monkeypox virus (MPXV) in the United States after a global outbreak in 2022, despite substantial declines in cases.
Design and population
Investigators analyzed surveillance data from the One CDC Data Platform and the National Notifiable Diseases Surveillance System for MPXV infections reported in the United States during 2024–2025.
Core methods
The analysis included case counts, demographic distributions, clade typing, and an assessment of vaccination status in relation to hospitalization outcomes using adjusted odds ratios.
Main results
In 2024, 2,803 MPXV cases were reported, including one travel-associated clade Ib case, while in 2025, 2,519 cases were reported, including 10 clade Ib cases. Cases in September–October 2025 were double those in the same period in 2024 but returned to background levels by December 2025. Among 860 patients with complete data, the odds of hospitalization were 9.73 times higher in unvaccinated individuals compared with those fully vaccinated after adjusting for age group and HIV status.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the resurgence of clade Ib cases and the doubling of late-2025 cases as evidence of ongoing transmission requiring sustained surveillance and targeted vaccination.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support prioritizing completion of the 2-dose vaccination series for persons at risk, including travelers to areas with clade I or II transmission, to reduce severe illness and hospitalization.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on passive surveillance data, which may undercount cases and lacks detailed clinical or genomic data for all reported cases, limiting causal inference and generalizability.

猴痘病毒分支特异性多重数字PCR检测方法在废水样本中的验证

Clade-specific multiplex digital PCR assay for monkeypox virus detection in spike-in wastewater samples.
作者： Melissa Pitton, Charles Gan, Patrick Schmidhalter, Christoph Ort, Timothy R Julian
摘要中文翻译猴痘病毒(MPXV)是一种人兽共患病原体,近期在非流行地区引发了猴痘暴发。基于废水的监测不依赖于个体检测,在监测猴痘病毒(MPXV)种群水平疾病动态方面具有显著潜力,因不同分支的传播能力和抗病毒药物敏感性存在差异。本研究报告了一种四重数字PCR检测方法的验证结果,该方法可检测和定量猴痘病毒的主要分支及亚分支。该检测方法在线性度、分析灵敏度(检测限在各靶标间为6.7至9.6拷贝/反应)以及在分支水平区分和定量猴痘病毒的特异性方面表现优异,在临床和废水应用中均具有优势。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
猴痘病毒(MPXV)是一种人兽共患病原体,近期在非流行地区引发了猴痘暴发。本研究旨在开发一种在废水样本中检测和定量猴痘病毒分支的方法。
设计与对象
本研究对一种四重数字PCR检测方法进行了验证,该方法可检测和定量猴痘病毒的主要分支及亚分支。该检测方法在添加猴痘病毒的废水样本中进行了测试,以确保其在监测种群水平疾病动态方面的有效性。
核心方法
该检测方法在添加猴痘病毒的废水样本中进行了验证,以评估其检测和定量猴痘病毒分支的能力。该方法在线性度、分析灵敏度以及在分支水平区分和定量猴痘病毒的特异性方面表现优异。
主要结果
该检测方法在线性度、分析灵敏度(检测限在各靶标间为6.7至9.6拷贝/反应)以及在分支水平区分和定量猴痘病毒的特异性方面表现优异。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为证明该检测方法在临床和废水应用中的潜力。其创新之处在于开发了一种猴痘病毒分支特异性多重数字PCR检测方法。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为基于废水的监测提供依据,用于监测猴痘病毒(MPXV)种群水平疾病动态,这对于理解不同分支间传播能力和抗病毒药物敏感性差异具有重要意义。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于添加猴痘病毒的废水样本,可能无法完全代表真实世界条件。然而,该检测方法在线性度、分析灵敏度和特异性方面表现优异,证据强度较高。

Clade-specific multiplex digital PCR assay for monkeypox virus detection in spike-in wastewater samples.

Authors: Melissa Pitton, Charles Gan, Patrick Schmidhalter, Christoph Ort, Timothy R Julian
Original abstract (English)
Monkeypox virus (MPXV) is a zoonotic pathogen that has recently caused outbreaks in non-endemic areas. Wastewater-based surveillance is independent of individual testing and offers substantial potential for monitoring population-level disease dynamics of MPXV at the clade level, as clades differ in transmissibility and mortality rate. Here, we report the validation of a four-plex digital PCR assay to detect and quantify the major clades and subclades of MPXV. The assay demonstrated strong linearity, analytical sensitivity (limits of detection ranged from 6.7 to 9.6 copies per reaction across targets) and specificity in distinguishing and quantifying MPXV by clade, which is advantageous for both clinical and wastewater applications.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Monkeypox virus (MPXV) is a zoonotic pathogen that has recently caused outbreaks in non-endemic areas. The study aimed to develop a method for detecting and quantifying MPXV clades in wastewater samples.
Design and population
The study involved the validation of a four-plex digital PCR assay to detect and quantify the major clades and subclades of MPXV. The assay was tested on spike-in wastewater samples to ensure its effectiveness in monitoring population-level disease dynamics.
Core methods
The assay was validated using spike-in wastewater samples to assess its ability to detect and quantify MPXV clades. It demonstrated strong linearity, analytical sensitivity, and specificity in distinguishing and quantifying MPXV by clade.
Main results
The assay demonstrated strong linearity, analytical sensitivity (limits of detection ranged from 6.7 to 9.6 copies per reaction across targets) and specificity in distinguishing and quantifying MPXV by clade.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as demonstrating the assay's potential for both clinical and wastewater applications. The novelty lies in the development of a clade-specific multiplex digital PCR assay for MPXV detection.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform wastewater-based surveillance for monitoring population-level disease dynamics of MPXV at the clade level, which is important for understanding transmissibility and mortality rate differences among clades.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to spike-in wastewater samples, which may not fully represent real-world conditions. The evidence is strong as the assay demonstrated strong linearity, analytical sensitivity, and specificity.

猴痘的皮肤镜特征:一项病例系列研究

Dermoscopic Features of Mpox: A Case Series
作者： Ilse Andrea Moreno‐Arquieta, Héctor Teodoro Rios‐Bazaldua, Roger Adrián González‐Ramírez, Gabriel Salerni, Erika Stephania Alvarado‐Arana, José Alberto García‐Lozano, Juan Pablo Gonzalez‐Morales, Jesus Alberto Cardenas‐de la Garza, Ilse Andrea Moreno-Arquieta, Héctor Teodoro Rios-Bazaldua
摘要中文翻译摘要 猴痘(猴痘)是一种由多形性皮肤损害和系统性症状(包括发热、淋巴结病、头痛和肌痛)为特征的病毒感染。由于其鉴别诊断范围广(诊断),准确识别需具备较高的临床疑似程度;在此背景下,皮肤镜可能成为提高诊断准确性的有用工具。我们回顾性纳入了2022年7月至2025年7月期间经RT-PCR确诊为猴痘且存在皮肤损害的6例患者。共纳入6例患者;其中3例(50%)在斑疹/水疱期接受评估,3例(50%)在结痂期接受评估。在斑疹/水疱期,共分析8处皮损;在结痂期,共分析5处皮损。在本系列研究中,斑疹/水疱期观察到粉红色-黄色无结构区域。识别这些皮肤镜特征可能提高临床疑似程度并支持诊断。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探索猴痘的皮肤镜特征,以提高诊断准确性,因其鉴别诊断范围广(诊断)且需精准识别。
设计与对象
本研究为一项回顾性病例系列研究,纳入2022年7月至2025年7月期间经RT-PCR确诊为猴痘且存在皮肤损害的6例患者。
核心方法
本研究通过皮肤镜分析斑疹/水疱期和结痂期中经RT-PCR确诊为猴痘患者的皮损。
主要结果
斑疹/水疱期共分析8处皮损,并观察到粉红色-黄色无结构区域。
解释与创新
所观察到的皮肤镜特征可能有助于提高临床疑似程度并支持猴痘的诊断。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为在快速且准确识别至关重要的环境中,将皮肤镜作为临床诊断猴痘的辅助工具提供依据。
局限与证据强度
本研究受限于样本量小且设计为回顾性,可能影响结果的普遍性。由于这些局限性,证据强度为中等。

Dermoscopic Features of Mpox: A Case Series

Authors: Ilse Andrea Moreno‐Arquieta, Héctor Teodoro Rios‐Bazaldua, Roger Adrián González‐Ramírez, Gabriel Salerni, Erika Stephania Alvarado‐Arana, José Alberto García‐Lozano, Juan Pablo Gonzalez‐Morales, Jesus Alberto Cardenas‐de la Garza, Ilse Andrea Moreno-Arquieta, Héctor Teodoro Rios-Bazaldua
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Monkeypox (mpox) is a viral infection characterized by polymorphic cutaneous lesions and systemic symptoms, including fever, lymphadenopathy, headache and myalgia. Given its broad differential diagnosis, accurate recognition requires a high level of clinical suspicion; in this context, dermoscopy represents a potentially useful tool to improve diagnostic accuracy. We conducted a retrospective case series of six patients with RT‐PCR–confirmed mpox and dermatologic involvement between July 2022 and July 2025. A total of six patients were included; three (50%) were evaluated in the macular/vesicular phase and three (50%) in the crusted phase. In the macular/vesicular phase, eight lesions were analysed. In the crusted phase, five lesions were analysed. In our series, we observed pinkish‐yellow structureless areas during the macular/vesicular phase. Recognition of these dermoscopic features may increase clinical suspicion and support the diagnosis.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to explore the dermoscopic features of mpox to improve diagnostic accuracy, given its broad differential diagnosis and the need for precise recognition.
Design and population
A retrospective case series was conducted involving six patients with RT-PCR–confirmed mpox and dermatologic involvement between July 2022 and July 2025.
Core methods
The study involved analysing lesions from patients with RT-PCR–confirmed mpox during the macular/vesicular and crusted phases using dermoscopy.
Main results
In the macular/vesicular phase, eight lesions were analysed, and pinkish-yellow structureless areas were observed.
Interpretation and novelty
The observed dermoscopic features may contribute to increasing clinical suspicion and supporting the diagnosis of mpox.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings may inform the use of dermoscopy as a supportive tool in the clinical diagnosis of mpox, particularly in settings where rapid and accurate identification is critical.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its small sample size and retrospective design, which may affect the generalisability of the results. The evidence is moderate in strength due to these limitations.

评估美国联邦人兽共患病记录与国家‘一体化健康’目标的符合性:基于COVID-19、猴痘与H5N1的多大语言模型评估

Evaluating U.S. Federal Zoonotic Records Against National One Health Goals: A Multi-LLM Assessment Across COVID-19, Mpox, and H5N1
作者： Bowen Long, Fangya Tan, Jinghang Zhang
摘要中文翻译(1) 背景:人兽共患病需要在人类、动物与环境健康部门间开展协调行动。尽管大量研究聚焦于暴发预测、预报与监测,但针对政府政策文件中‘一体化健康’原则体现情况的系统性评估关注较少。(2) 方法:本研究构建多大语言模型(LLM)框架,评估了477份美国政府文件,涵盖COVID-19、猴痘与高致病性禽流感(H5N1)。采用包含19个评分项的评估量表,对美国‘国家一体化健康框架’的三大目标进行评估:协调与沟通、暴发应对与监测。Claude、GPT与Gemini三个模型独立评估文件,采用Fleiss κ系数评估一致性,并由人工进行独立验证。(3) 结果:整体‘一体化健康’对齐程度有限,无任何疾病×文件类型×目标组合的对齐率超过39%。文件类型差异最为显著:在协调与暴发应对方面,机构技术指导文件的对齐率分别比《联邦公报》文件高13至30个百分点(p值均<0.001)。疾病间差异不一致。在排除一项对措辞敏感的评分项后,三个LLM在87.1%的文件-评分项评估中达成一致。人类-模型一致性(Cohen κ=0.513–0.692)与人类-人类一致性(κ=0.649)相当。(4) 结论:研究得出三项主要发现:文件类型在‘一体化健康’对齐程度上较疾病或发布时间更显著;更清晰与可操作的评估标准可提升一致性;多LLM共识在可扩展政策评估中表现出较强的可重复性。这些发现将人兽共患病研究从暴发预测与监测扩展至‘一体化健康’政策的系统性评估。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估美国联邦人兽共患病记录与‘国家一体化健康’目标的符合程度,重点关注猴痘背景下政府政策文件中‘一体化健康’原则的体现情况。
设计与对象
本研究采用多大语言模型框架,评估了477份美国政府文件,涵盖猴痘、COVID-19与H5N1。文件类型包括机构技术指导文件与《联邦公报》条目。
核心方法
评估采用包含19个评分项的量表,涵盖‘一体化健康’的三大目标:协调与沟通、暴发应对与监测。Claude、GPT与Gemini三个模型独立分析文件,采用Fleiss κ系数评估一致性,并由人工审核员进行验证。
主要结果
整体‘一体化健康’对齐程度有限,无任何疾病×文件类型×目标组合的对齐率超过39%。文件类型差异最为显著:在协调与暴发应对方面,机构技术指导文件的对齐率分别比《联邦公报》文件高13至30个百分点(p值均<0.001)。
解释与创新
研究发现,文件类型对‘一体化健康’对齐程度的影响较疾病或发布时间更显著。研究还指出,更清晰与可操作的评估标准可提升评估一致性,且多大语言模型共识在可扩展政策评估中表现出较强的可重复性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,系统性评估‘一体化健康’政策文件可提升跨部门协调,并强化人兽共患病(如猴痘)政策目标的可操作性。
局限与证据强度
本研究基于多大语言模型框架,可能存在解释偏倚。但模型间高度一致性及人工验证结果支持研究结论的中等可信度。

Evaluating U.S. Federal Zoonotic Records Against National One Health Goals: A Multi-LLM Assessment Across COVID-19, Mpox, and H5N1

Authors: Bowen Long, Fangya Tan, Jinghang Zhang
Original abstract (English)
(1) Background: Zoonotic diseases require coordinated action across human, animal, and environmental health sectors. While substantial research has focused on outbreak prediction, forecasting, and surveillance, less attention has been given to systematically evaluating how One Health principles are reflected in government policy documents. (2) Methods: We developed a multi–large language model (LLM) framework to evaluate 477 U.S. government documents covering COVID-19, mpox, and highly pathogenic avian influenza (H5N1). Agency technical guidance and Federal Register documents were evaluated using a 19-item rubric covering three goals of the U.S. National One Health Framework: coordination and communication, outbreak response, and surveillance. Claude, GPT, and Gemini independently evaluated the documents, with agreement assessed using Fleiss’ κ and independent human validation. (3) Results: One Health alignment was generally limited, with no disease × document-type × goal combination exceeding 39%. Document type showed the clearest differences: agency technical guidance had 13 to 30 percentage-point higher alignment than Federal Register documents for coordination and outbreak response, respectively (both p < 0.001). Differences across diseases were less consistent. After excluding one wording-sensitive rubric item, the three LLMs reached unanimous agreement in 87.1% of document-item evaluations. Human–model agreement (Cohen’s κ = 0.513–0.692) was comparable to human–human agreement (κ = 0.649). (4) Conclusions: Three main findings emerged: document type showed clearer differences in One Health alignment than disease or publication period; clearer and more operational criteria supported more consistent evaluation; and multi-LLM consensus showed strong reproducibility for scalable policy assessment. Together, these findings extend zoonotic-disease research beyond outbreak prediction and surveillance toward systematic evaluation of One Health policy.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate how well U.S. federal zoonotic records align with the National One Health goals, particularly in the context of mpox, by examining the reflection of these principles in government policy documents.
Design and population
The study involved a multi-LLM framework to assess 477 U.S. government documents related to mpox, COVID-19, and H5N1. These documents included agency technical guidance and Federal Register entries.
Core methods
The evaluation used a 19-item rubric to assess alignment with three One Health goals: coordination and communication, outbreak response, and surveillance. The three LLMs (Claude, GPT, and Gemini) independently analyzed the documents, with agreement measured using Fleiss’ κ and validated by human reviewers.
Main results
One Health alignment was generally limited, with no disease × document-type × goal combination exceeding 39%. Document type showed the clearest differences, with agency technical guidance having 13 to 30 percentage-point higher alignment than Federal Register documents for coordination and outbreak response, respectively (both p < 0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that document type had a more significant impact on One Health alignment than disease or publication period. It also highlighted that clearer and more operational criteria improve evaluation consistency, and multi-LLM consensus demonstrates strong reproducibility for scalable policy assessment.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that systematic evaluation of One Health policy documents can improve coordination across sectors and enhance the operationalization of policy goals for zoonotic diseases like mpox.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence is based on a multi-LLM framework and may be subject to interpretation biases. However, the high agreement between models and human validation supports moderate confidence in the findings.

血浆多组学分析揭示猴痘和猴痘-HIV共感染中脂质-免疫失调及外泌体重塑。

Multiomics profiling of plasma reveals lipid-immune dysregulation and exosome remodeling in mpox and mpox-HIV co-infection.
作者： Siqi Yong, Jiaying Zhang, He Tian, Zehua Wang, Shaohua Zhang, Robin Barry Chan, Yingjuan Zhou, Guibin Wang, Lianchun Liang, Zhifeng Song
摘要中文翻译背景:猴痘病毒(MPXV)感染多种人类细胞类型,且人类免疫缺陷病毒(HIV)共感染较为常见。MPXV感染的免疫代谢后果以及HIV如何改变这些过程仍不明确。方法:我们在一项发现队列研究(n = 81)中进行了定量血浆脂质组学和精确代谢组学分析,该队列包括MPXV单感染(MPLWOH)、MPXV-HIV共感染(MPLWH)和HIV单感染(PLWH)患者以及健康对照组,并整合了外泌体蛋白组学、细胞因子谱分析以及外泌体处理的HepG2和A549细胞的转录组学数据,以进行功能解释。独立验证队列研究(n = 65)用于评估跨队列研究的可重复性。发现:MPXV感染导致广泛的脂质重塑,表现为磷脂酰丝氨酸(PS)和磷脂酰乙醇胺(PE)升高,而磷脂酰胆碱(PC)、溶血磷脂、胆固醇酯(CE)以及外泌体中的卵磷脂胆固醇乙酰转移酶(LCAT)和脂蛋白脂肪酶(LPL)水平降低。这些脂质变化与组织损伤标志物和炎症细胞因子相关。MPLWH组表现出更严重的代谢紊乱,包括显著的硫酸脑苷脂(SL)减少、胆固醇和高密度脂蛋白胆固醇(HDL-c)水平降低,以及脂质-细胞因子关联的广泛重塑。MPLWH组中SL的减少与外泌体中COPI介导的逆行运输蛋白的丰度相关。外泌体处理细胞的转录组学分析提供了功能验证:MPLWOH外泌体诱导与脂质代谢和修复相关的上皮程序,而MPLWH外泌体则驱动磷脂重塑及急性炎症和黏膜屏障应激反应。结论:MPXV感染重新编程了宿主的脂质代谢和外泌体组成,HIV共感染则放大了炎症、代谢和运输的紊乱。这些一致的多组学特征将系统性脂质失调与外泌体介导的免疫调节联系起来,并指出了针对宿主的潜在干预靶点。资助:本研究由广州国家实验室重大专项资助。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究探讨了猴痘病毒(MPXV)感染的免疫代谢后果,以及人类免疫缺陷病毒(HIV)共感染如何改变这些过程,尽管MPXV具有广泛的细胞嗜性且HIV共感染较为常见,但这些过程此前仍未明确。
设计与对象
本研究在一项发现队列研究中进行了定量血浆脂质组学和代谢组学分析,该队列包括81名参与者,涵盖MPXV单感染、MPXV-HIV共感染、HIV单感染患者以及健康对照组,并使用独立验证队列研究(n = 65)评估结果的可重复性。
核心方法
进行了血浆脂质组学和代谢组学分析,并结合外泌体蛋白组学、细胞因子谱分析以及外泌体处理的HepG2和A549细胞的转录组学分析,以功能化解释研究结果。
主要结果
MPXV感染导致广泛的脂质重塑,表现为磷脂酰丝氨酸和磷脂酰乙醇胺升高,而磷脂酰胆碱、溶血磷脂、胆固醇酯以及外泌体中的卵磷脂胆固醇乙酰转移酶(LCAT)和脂蛋白脂肪酶(LPL)水平降低,这些变化与组织损伤标志物和炎症细胞因子相关。
解释与创新
作者认为,MPXV感染重新编程了宿主的脂质代谢和外泌体组成,HIV共感染则放大了炎症、代谢和运输的紊乱,并识别出将系统性脂质失调与外泌体介导的免疫调节联系起来的一致多组学特征。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,针对宿主的干预措施可能成为缓解猴痘和猴痘-HIV共感染中免疫代谢失调的潜在靶点,为未来的治疗和诊断策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性包括缺乏纵向采样和体内功能验证,这限制了因果推断和结果的普遍性,因此证据的可信度为中等。

Multiomics profiling of plasma reveals lipid-immune dysregulation and exosome remodeling in mpox and mpox-HIV co-infection.

Authors: Siqi Yong, Jiaying Zhang, He Tian, Zehua Wang, Shaohua Zhang, Robin Barry Chan, Yingjuan Zhou, Guibin Wang, Lianchun Liang, Zhifeng Song
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Monkeypox virus (MPXV) infects diverse human cell types, and human immunodeficiency virus (HIV) co-infection is common. The immunometabolic consequences of MPXV infection, and how it may be altered by HIV, remain poorly defined. METHODS: We performed quantitative plasma lipidomics and precise metabolomics in a discovery cohort (n = 81) comprising MPXV-monoinfected (MPLWOH), MPXV-HIV-coinfected (MPLWH), and HIV-monoinfected (PLWH) patients and healthy controls, integrating exosome proteomics, cytokine profiling, and transcriptomics of exosome-treated HepG2 and A549 cells for functional interpretation. An independent validation cohort (n = 65) was used to assess cross-cohort reproducibility. FINDINGS: MPXV infection induced broad lipid remodeling, with elevations in phosphatidylserine (PS) and phosphatidylethanolamine (PE) and reductions in phosphatidylcholine (PC), lysophospholipids, cholesteryl ester (CE), and exosomal lecithin-cholesterol acyltransferase (LCAT) and lipoprotein lipase (LPL). These lipid alterations were correlated with tissue injury markers and inflammatory cytokines. The MPLWH group exhibited more severe metabolic disruption, including marked sulfatide (SL) depletion, lower cholesterol and high-density lipoprotein cholesterol (HDL-c), and extensive rewiring of lipid-cytokine associations. SL depletion in MPLWH correlated with abundances of COPI-mediated retrograde trafficking proteins in exosomes. Transcriptomic profiling of exosome-treated cells provided functional validation: MPLWOH exosomes induced lipid metabolism and repair-associated epithelial programs, while MPLWH exosomes drove phospholipid remodeling and acute inflammatory and mucosal barrier-stress responses. CONCLUSIONS: MPXV infection reprograms host lipid metabolism and exosome composition, with HIV co-infection amplifying inflammatory, metabolic, and trafficking disruptions. These convergent multi-omics signatures link systemic lipid dysregulation to exosome-mediated immunomodulation and identify potential targets for host-directed interventions. FUNDING: This study was funded by the Major Project of Guangzhou National Laboratory.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the immunometabolic consequences of monkeypox virus (MPXV) infection and how human immunodeficiency virus (HIV) co-infection may alter these processes, which were previously poorly defined despite MPXV’s broad cellular tropism and the common occurrence of HIV co-infection.
Design and population
The study performed quantitative plasma lipidomics and metabolomics in a discovery cohort of 81 participants including MPXV-monoinfected, MPXV-HIV-coinfected, HIV-monoinfected patients, and healthy controls, with an independent validation cohort of 65 participants to assess reproducibility.
Core methods
Plasma lipidomics and metabolomics were conducted, alongside exosome proteomics, cytokine profiling, and transcriptomics of exosome-treated HepG2 and A549 cells to functionally interpret the findings.
Main results
MPXV infection induced broad lipid remodeling with elevations in phosphatidylserine and phosphatidylethanolamine and reductions in phosphatidylcholine, lysophospholipids, cholesteryl ester, and exosomal LCAT and LPL, and these alterations correlated with tissue injury markers and inflammatory cytokines.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that MPXV infection reprograms host lipid metabolism and exosome composition, with HIV co-infection amplifying inflammatory, metabolic, and trafficking disruptions, and identified convergent multi-omics signatures linking systemic lipid dysregulation to exosome-mediated immunomodulation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest potential targets for host-directed interventions to mitigate immunometabolic dysregulation in mpox and mpox-HIV co-infection, informing future therapeutic and diagnostic strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s limitations include the absence of longitudinal sampling and functional in vivo validation, which constrains causal inference and generalizability, warranting moderate confidence in the evidence.

猴痘病毒IIb分支分离株在多种小鼠模型中表现出比IIa分支分离株更低的致病性

Monkeypox virus clade IIb isolate exhibits reduced virulence relative to clade IIa isolates in multiple murine models
作者： Stephanie V. Trefry, Santiago Vidal-Freire, Mayanka Awasthi, Abel D. Ordonez, Brett P. Eaton, Randy J. Hart, Christy N. Raney, Amy L. Cregger, Chase A. Gonzales, Robert N. Enamorado
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
猴痘病是由具有广泛差异病例死亡率的猴痘病毒(MPXV)不同亚分支引起的新兴人类疾病,但由于缺乏合适的小型动物模型,亚分支之间的致病性差异仍不完全理解。
设计与对象
本研究评估了四种小鼠模型——CAST-EiJ和三种免疫缺陷品系(C57BL/6 Ifnar −/− 、C57BL/6 Ifngr −/− 以及C57BL/6 Ifnar −/− / Ifngr −/− )——对MPXV IIa分支(WR 7-61和US-2003)和IIb分支(MA-2022)分离株的易感性。
核心方法
作者使用标准化剂量感染小鼠,并通过包括体温下降、体重减轻和死亡在内的临床症状监测疾病进展。
主要结果
所有四种小鼠模型在感染IIa分支后均表现出严重疾病,而IIb分支感染在相似剂量下仅引起轻微至轻度疾病;仅在最易感的免疫缺陷小鼠模型C57BL/6 Ifnar −/− / Ifngr −/− 中,IIb分支分离株在最高剂量(8.0 log10 PFU)下导致了40%的死亡率。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,在小鼠模型中,IIa分支的致病性大约是IIb分支的100到100,000倍,并首次在小鼠中观察到IIb分支的致死性感染,识别了三种新的MPXV研究模型。
科研与公卫意义
这些小鼠模型能够快速表征新兴的MPXV亚分支,并促进应对措施的开发,填补了猴痘病研究基础设施中的关键空白。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于仅使用了小鼠模型,这可能无法完全模拟人类疾病,且缺乏对IIb或I分支分离株的数据,限制了其对所有MPXV亚分支的普遍适用性。

Monkeypox virus clade IIb isolate exhibits reduced virulence relative to clade IIa isolates in multiple murine models

Authors: Stephanie V. Trefry, Santiago Vidal-Freire, Mayanka Awasthi, Abel D. Ordonez, Brett P. Eaton, Randy J. Hart, Christy N. Raney, Amy L. Cregger, Chase A. Gonzales, Robert N. Enamorado
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Monkeypox virus (MPXV) is the causative agent of mpox disease in humans. The virus comprises two clades, Central African clade I and West African clade II, with case fatality rates of ~11 and ~4%, respectively. Since the discovery of mpox disease in 1970, the virus has been restricted to Africa. However, in 2022, a previously unrecognized subclade IIb caused the largest global outbreak of mpox disease with a case fatality rate of ~0.2%. The difference in virulence of MPXV subclades in human infection warrants further investigation; however, one critical limitation is the lack of susceptible small animal models. In this study, we investigated the susceptibility of four murine models, including CAST-EiJ and three immunocompromised models (C57BL/6 Ifnar −/− , C57BL/6 Ifngr −/− , and C57BL/6 Ifnar −/− / Ifngr −/− ) to MPXV clade IIa (WR 7-61 and US-2003) and IIb (MA-2022) isolates. All four mouse models were susceptible to clade IIa infection, leading to severe disease marked by decreased body temperature, weight loss, and lethality. In contrast, clade IIb infection produced minimal to mild disease at similar doses in all four murine models. The clade IIb isolate produced severe disease (40% lethality) at only the highest dose (8.0 log 10 PFU) in the most susceptible immunocompromised mouse model, C57BL/6 Ifnar −/− / Ifngr −/− . This is the first demonstration of lethal disease with clade IIb in a murine model. In addition, these data show that clade IIa is ~100 to 100,000-fold more virulent than clade IIb and provides three additional murine models for investigating MPXV infection and pathogenesis. IMPORTANCE Mpox is an emerging human disease caused by four distinct MPXV subclades (Ia, Ib, IIa, and IIb). Despite genetic similarities, the case fatality rate varies considerably between the subclades: Ia (~11%), Ib and IIa (~4%), and IIb (~0.2%). Since 2022, multiple mpox outbreaks have occurred due to previously unrecognized subclades, leading to the declaration of two public health emergencies by the World Health Organization. This unprecedented global spread, coupled with the variation in the severity of human disease, underscores the importance of research into the pathogenesis of emerging MPXV subclades. However, a critical limitation is the lack of suitable small animal models. This study identifies three additional murine models susceptible to MPXV clade II infection and demonstrates significant virulence differences between clades IIa and IIb. These models will enable a rapid characterization of previously unrecognized subclades and will facilitate countermeasure development.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Mpox is an emerging human disease caused by distinct Monkeypox virus (MPXV) subclades with widely varying case fatality rates, yet the virulence differences between subclades remain insufficiently understood due to the lack of suitable small animal models.
Design and population
The study evaluated susceptibility of four murine models—CAST-EiJ and three immunocompromised strains (C57BL/6 Ifnar −/− , C57BL/6 Ifngr −/− , and C57BL/6 Ifnar −/− / Ifngr −/− )—to MPXV clade IIa (WR 7-61 and US-2003) and clade IIb (MA-2022) isolates.
Core methods
The authors infected mice with standardized doses of clade IIa and IIb isolates and monitored disease progression through clinical signs including body temperature, weight loss, and lethality.
Main results
All four mouse models developed severe disease after clade IIa infection, whereas clade IIb infection produced minimal to mild disease at similar doses; only the most immunocompromised model (C57BL/6 Ifnar −/− / Ifngr −/− ) showed 40% lethality at the highest clade IIb dose (8.0 log10 PFU).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate clade IIa is approximately 100- to 100,000-fold more virulent than clade IIb in murine models and provide the first lethal clade IIb infection in mice, identifying three new models for MPXV research.
Scientific and public-health significance
These murine models enable rapid characterization of emerging MPXV subclades and facilitate countermeasure development, addressing a critical gap in mpox research infrastructure.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its exclusive use of murine models, which may not fully recapitulate human disease, and by the absence of data on clade Ib or clade I isolates, reducing generalisability to all MPXV subclades.

猴痘:猴痘的一般特征及在该人兽共患病毒病背景下的“同一健康”方法应用

MPOX: ASPECTOS GERAIS E O USO DA ABORDAGEM ONE HEALTH NO CONTEXTO DESSA ZOONOSE VIRAL MPOX: GENERAL ASPECTS AND THE USE OF THE ONE HEALTH APPROACH IN THE CONTEXT OF THIS VIRAL ZOONOSIS MPOX: ASPECTOS GENERALES Y USO DEL ENFOQUE UNA SOLA SALUD EN EL CONTEXTO DE ESTA ZOONOSIS VIRAL
作者： Valesca Pinto, Fellipe Siller Thebaldi, Igor Rosa Meurer
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
猴痘(猴痘)是一种由猴痘病毒(MPXV)引起的全球分布的新发人兽共患病毒病,该病毒属于正痘病毒属(正痘病毒)。
设计与对象
本研究旨在简要描述猴痘的主要特征,并介绍“同一健康”方法在应对此类人兽共患病中的应用信息。
核心方法
主要通过直接接触损伤和分泌物传播,确诊需通过PCR检测。
主要结果
猴痘与行为和社会因素相关,影响特定人群,亟需有效的预防措施及公共政策。
解释与创新
在该人兽共患病背景下,“同一健康”方法通过整合人类、动物及环境健康行动,具有重要意义。
科研与公卫意义
跨学科合作与加强流行病学监测是通过“同一健康”方法有效控制猴痘的关键。
局限与证据强度
猴痘分为前驱期和发疹期,以发热、淋巴结病和皮肤损伤为特征。

MPOX: ASPECTOS GERAIS E O USO DA ABORDAGEM ONE HEALTH NO CONTEXTO DESSA ZOONOSE VIRAL MPOX: GENERAL ASPECTS AND THE USE OF THE ONE HEALTH APPROACH IN THE CONTEXT OF THIS VIRAL ZOONOSIS MPOX: ASPECTOS GENERALES Y USO DEL ENFOQUE UNA SOLA SALUD EN EL CONTEXTO DE ESTA ZOONOSIS VIRAL

Authors: Valesca Pinto, Fellipe Siller Thebaldi, Igor Rosa Meurer
Original abstract (English)
A Mpox é uma zoonose viral emergente de distribuição global, causada pelo Monkeypox vírus (MPXV), pertencente ao gênero Orthopoxvirus. O presente estudo teve como objetivo descrever de forma breve e pontual as principais características da Mpox e apresentar informações sobre o uso da abordagem One Health como forma de enfrentamento a essa zoonose. Trata-se de uma revisão narrativa da literatura, com seleção de publicações realizadas entre 2014 e 2025, utilizando as bases de dados LILACS, SciELO, PubMed e Google Acadêmico. Além disso, documentos de órgãos oficiais nacionais e internacionais foram pesquisados no contexto do estudo. O MPXV é um vírus de DNA de fita dupla, identificado inicialmente em animais em 1958 e em humanos na década de 1970, apresentando dois clados com potencial para causar infecções. Clinicamente, a Mpox manifesta-se em fases prodrômica e eruptiva, caracterizadas por febre, linfadenopatia e lesões cutâneas. A transmissão ocorre principalmente por contato direto com lesões e secreções, sendo o diagnóstico confirmado por PCR. O manejo da doença inclui cuidados relacionados aos sintomas e tratamento das complicações secundárias. A abordagem One Health, ao integrar ações entre saúde humana, animal e ambiental, torna-se relevante no contexto dessa zoonose. A Mpox apresenta relação com fatores comportamentais e sociais, impactando grupos específicos, necessitando de medidas de prevenção e políticas públicas efetivas. A cooperação interdisciplinar e o fortalecimento da vigilância epidemiológica são fundamentais para o controle efetivo da Mpox a partir da abordagem One Health.

A Mpox manifesta-se em fases prodrômica e eruptiva, caracterizadas por febre, linfadenopatia e lesões cutâneas.

利用预训练迁移学习网络提升自动化人类猴痘皮肤病变检测的可解释性

Enhancing Interpretability for Automated Human Monkeypox Skin Lesions Detection Using Pre-trained Transfer Learning Networks
作者： Kanak Kumar, Anshul Verma, Pradeepika Verma
摘要中文翻译摘要 由于图像对比度低、疾病表现变异大以及皮肤病变在视觉上的高度相似性,皮肤病变识别仍是一项具有挑战性的任务。深度迁移学习(TL)集成人工智能(AI)系统为多种传染性皮肤病的快速准确计算机辅助诊断提供了有效解决方案。本研究提出了一种基于TL的框架,用于自动化人类猴痘(MP)皮肤病变检测(HMSLD),以对猴痘(MP)、水痘(CP)、麻疹(MS)及正常皮肤病变进行分类。该研究使用了一个公开可用的数据集,包含654张RGB临床图像。HMSLD框架进行了图像预处理、数据增强、通过逆类权重处理类别不平衡,并对预训练TL模型(包括Xception、ResNet152V2、NASNetLarge、EfficientNetB0、EfficientNetB7、MobileNetV2和MobileNetV3)进行了比较分析。MobileNetV3在所有模型中表现最佳。其整体性能为:准确率=91%,精确率=91%,召回率=89%,F1值=90%,AUROC为0.99,表明具有优异的判别能力。MobileNetV3在麻疹检测中实现了96%的精确率,在正常皮肤检测中实现了100%的召回率,而EfficientNetB0在精确率上表现最佳(96%)且F1值为93%。MobileNetV3在所评估模型中提供了分类准确性、鲁棒性与计算效率之间最为平衡的权衡。这些评估性能表明HMSLD框架具有潜在应用价值,可能成为一种可靠且具有成本效益的计算机辅助诊断工具,用于早期猴痘筛查及其他临床相似皮肤疾病。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在解决由猴痘病毒引起的皮肤病变识别挑战,该挑战因图像对比度低、疾病表现变异大以及与其他皮肤病变在视觉上的高度相似性而复杂化。
设计与对象
本研究评估了一种基于迁移学习的框架,用于利用包含654张RGB临床图像的公开数据集对人类猴痘皮肤病变进行自动检测。该框架用于对猴痘、水痘、麻疹及正常皮肤病变进行分类。
核心方法
该框架采用了图像预处理、数据增强、通过逆类权重处理类别不平衡,并对预训练迁移学习模型(包括Xception、ResNet152V2、NASNetLarge、EfficientNetB0、EfficientNetB7、MobileNetV2和MobileNetV3)进行了比较分析。
主要结果
MobileNetV3在麻疹检测中实现了最高精确率(96%)和正常皮肤检测中100%的召回率,而EfficientNetB0在精确率上表现最佳(96%)且F1值为93%。该框架的整体性能为:准确率91%、精确率91%、召回率89%、F1值90%,AUROC为0.99。
解释与创新
结果表明,所提出的HMSLD框架具有优异的判别能力,可能成为一种可靠且具有成本效益的诊断工具,用于早期猴痘筛查及其他类似皮肤疾病。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,HMSLD框架有望提升猴痘及其他临床相似皮肤疾病的诊断准确性与效率,支持早期检测及改善公共卫生监测。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于摘要层面的分析,且由于缺乏详细的方法与结果,证据强度为中等。

Enhancing Interpretability for Automated Human Monkeypox Skin Lesions Detection Using Pre-trained Transfer Learning Networks

Authors: Kanak Kumar, Anshul Verma, Pradeepika Verma
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Identification of skin lesions remains a challenging task because of low image contrast, large variations in disease manifestations, and strong visual similarities among dermatological conditions. Deep transfer learning (TL)-integrated artificial intelligence (AI) systems offer an effective solution for rapid and accurate computer-aided diagnosis of multiple infectious skin diseases. In this study, a TL-based framework is proposed as an automated human monkeypox (MP) skin lesion detection (HMSLD) for monkeypox (MP), chickenpox (CP), measles (MS), and normal skin lesion classification. A publicly available dataset comprising 654 RGB clinical images. The HMSLD performed image preprocessing, data augmentation, class imbalance handling through inverse class weighting, and comparative analysis of pre-trained TL models, namely Xception, ResNet152V2, NASNetLarge, EfficientNetB0, EfficientNetB7, MobileNetV2, and MobileNetV3. MobileNetV3 performed best among the models. Their overall performance with an accuracy = 91%, precision = 91%, recall = 89%, F1-score = 90%, and an AUROC of 0.99, indicating excellent discriminative capability. MobileNetV3 achieved 96% precision for measles and 100% recall for normal skin, while EfficientNetB0 performed the highest precision at 96% and an F1-score of 93%. MobileNetV3 provides the most balanced trade-off among the evaluated models between classification accuracy, robustness, and computational efficiency. These evaluated performances indicate the potential of the HMSLD framework. It may be a reliable and cost-effective computer-aided diagnostic tool for early MP screening and other clinically similar skin diseases.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenge of identifying skin lesions caused by monkeypox virus, which is complicated by low image contrast, large variations in disease manifestations, and strong visual similarities with other dermatological conditions.
Design and population
This study evaluated a transfer learning-based framework for automated detection of human monkeypox skin lesions using a publicly available dataset comprising 654 RGB clinical images. The framework was tested for classification of monkeypox, chickenpox, measles, and normal skin lesions.
Core methods
The framework employed image preprocessing, data augmentation, class imbalance handling through inverse class weighting, and comparative analysis of pre-trained transfer learning models including Xception, ResNet152V2, NASNetLarge, EfficientNetB0, EfficientNetB7, MobileNetV2, and MobileNetV3.
Main results
MobileNetV3 achieved the highest precision at 96% for measles and 100% recall for normal skin, while EfficientNetB0 had the highest precision at 96% and an F1-score of 93%. The overall performance of the framework was 91% accuracy, 91% precision, 89% recall, and 90% F1-score, with an AUROC of 0.99.
Interpretation and novelty
The results suggest that the proposed HMSLD framework has excellent discriminative capability and may serve as a reliable and cost-effective diagnostic tool for early monkeypox screening and other similar skin diseases.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings indicate that the HMSLD framework could enhance the accuracy and efficiency of diagnosing monkeypox and other clinically similar skin diseases, supporting early detection and improved public health surveillance.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an abstract-only analysis, and the evidence strength is moderate due to the lack of detailed methodology and results beyond the abstract.

纽约市健康 + 医院(纽约市大型城市安全网医疗系统)中 猴痘 和 疫苗接种 的226种模式与差距(2022-2025年)

226 Patterns and Gaps in Mpox Vaccination at NYC Health + Hospitals, a Large Urban Safety Net Health System in New York City, 2022-2025
作者： AlShanqeeti S, Krishnan G, Baghdadi J, Goodman K, Schrank G, Pineles L, Goedken C, Coffey K, Leekha S, Harris A
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估生成式人工智能(GenAI)在评估医院相关菌血症和真菌血症(HOB)事件的归因及可预防性方面的应用价值。
设计与对象
本研究为一项回顾性 队列研究 研究,纳入了美国11家退伍军人事务管理局(VA)医院中血培养阳性的住院患者,并随机纳入了住院第3天之后采集的阳性血培养样本。
核心方法
GenAI(OpenAI GPT-4o)被提示使用标准化提示及临床数据,提供最可能的感染来源并判定 HOB 的可预防性。感染预防专家基于 GenAI 输出结果提供 GenAI 辅助的感染来源及可预防性判定,另一名感染预防专家对随机子集进行了单独人工审查。
主要结果
GenAI most commonly attributed bacteremia to gastrointestinal (GI) sources (14/52, 26.9%), followed by genitourinary (GU) (12/52, 23%), central line (CLABSI) (8/52, 15%), and skin and soft tissue infections (SSTI) (7/52, 14%) sources. IP-AI determination of source agreed with GenAI source in 67% (35/52) of cases, while GenAI adjudication of preventability matched IP-AI adjudication in 20/52 (39%).
解释与创新
作者得出结论:感染预防专家在 HOB 感染来源判定上常与 GenAI 一致,但在可预防性评估上存在分歧,这表明 GenAI 可协助识别感染来源,但在可预防性评估方面可能不可靠。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,将 GenAI 集成至 HOB 感染预防工作中,可提高感染来源识别速度,但必须结合专家判断进行可预防性评估,这对感染预防策略至关重要。
局限与证据强度
本研究受限于对可预防性判定依赖 GenAI,其结果可能与专家意见不一致,且分析的 HOB 事件样本量较小。由于上述局限性,证据强度为中等。

226 Patterns and Gaps in Mpox Vaccination at NYC Health + Hospitals, a Large Urban Safety Net Health System in New York City, 2022-2025

Authors: AlShanqeeti S, Krishnan G, Baghdadi J, Goodman K, Schrank G, Pineles L, Goedken C, Coffey K, Leekha S, Harris A
Original abstract (English)
In this study, we analyze microbiological testing data from more than 200,000 healthcare-associated water system samples collected since September 2019, comparing pathogen positivity across ice machines, gen- eral potable water, and cooling towers. Results demonstrate substantial variability in pathogen positivity by system type, with NTM showing mark- edly higher positivity in ice machines and potable water systems (relative to traditional indicator organisms). These findings indicate that uniform, organism-agnostic surveillance strategies are insufficient and that WMPs must be selectively designed based on sys- tem type, exposure route, and patient risk. Methods This is a retrospective cohort study of hospitalized patients with positive blood cultures at 11 US Veterans Affairs (VA) hospitals. A separate human-only review by a different IP expert was performed (IP-alone) on a random subset to com- pare time required for review. Results We included 52 HOB events for IP- AI and a subset of 21 for IP-alone. The median time to complete an IP-AI review was 11.5 (IQR 6 – 20.5) minutes compared to a median of 25 (IQR 13 – 33) minutes for IP-alone. Conclusion IP experts often agreed with GenAI for source of HOB but disagreed with its preventability. However, healthcare water systems harbor a wide range of opportunistic premise plumbing pathogens (OPPPs), including nontuberculous mycobacteria (NTM), P...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the use of generative artificial intelligence (GenAI) in assessing the attribution and preventability of hospital-onset bacteremia and fungemia (HOB) events.
Design and population
This is a retrospective cohort study of hospitalized patients with positive blood cultures at 11 US Veterans Affairs (VA) hospitals, including a random sample of positive blood cultures collected after day 3 of admission.
Core methods
GenAI (OpenAI GPT-4o) was prompted to provide the most likely source and adjudicate the preventability of HOB using standardized prompts and clinical data. IP experts used GenAI output to provide GenAI-assisted determinations of source and preventability, and a separate human-only review by a different IP expert was performed on a random subset.
Main results
GenAI most commonly attributed bacteremia to gastrointestinal (GI) sources (14/52, 26.9%), followed by genitourinary (GU) (12/52, 23%), central line (CLABSI) (8/52, 15%), and skin and soft tissue infections (SSTI) (7/52, 14%) sources. IP-AI determination of source agreed with GenAI source in 67% (35/52) of cases, while GenAI adjudication of preventability matched IP-AI adjudication in 20/52 (39%).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that IP experts often agreed with GenAI for the source of HOB but disagreed with its preventability, indicating that while GenAI can assist in identifying sources, it may not be reliable for assessing preventability.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that integrating GenAI into HOB surveillance could enhance the speed of source identification but must be complemented by expert judgment for preventability assessments, which is critical for infection prevention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on GenAI for preventability adjudication, which may not align with expert opinions, and the small sample size of HOB events analyzed. The evidence is moderate due to these limitations.

指动式微流控设备用于即时检测循环无细胞核酸提取

Finger-actuated microfluidic device for point-of-care circulating cell-free nucleic acid extraction.
作者： Ghulam Musaddaq, Labibah Ehsan, Yano Yoga, Joshua Castillo, Linda Marriott, Nicholas R. Leslie, Deborah A. Williamson, Thomas Tran, Chuan Kok Lim, Maïwenn Kersaudy-Kerhoas
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究针对即时检测点快速、可及的核酸提取需求,以支持分散式分子诊断,特别是临床样本中低丰度循环无细胞核酸的检测。
设计与对象
作者开发并评估了一种指动式微流控设备,旨在实现无需电源、无需移液器的核酸提取,采用手动、无仪器工作流程。
核心方法
该设备集成了指动阀、泵和泡囊式试剂储存,在约35分钟内完成裂解、结合、洗涤和洗脱,并与商用磁珠法手动台式提取及自动化机器人工作流程进行了对比基准测试。
主要结果
在DNA添加的水样本(5–20 ng mL−1)中,该设备的回收率为84.6%,而台式对照为90.7%;在人源混合血浆样本中,初始样本体积为100、75和50 μL时,该设备的回收率分别比台式提取对照高18%、62%和552%。
解释与创新
作者将该设备解释为一种可扩展的分散式核酸提取解决方案,其性能接近标准方案,通过携带巨细胞病毒、猴痘病毒或基孔肯雅病毒阳性患者样本的试点验证,成功实现了qPCR扩增。
科研与公卫意义
该设备有助于弥合基于侧流核酸扩增技术与分散式诊断之间的差距,在基础设施和电力受限的资源有限环境下支持传染病管理。
局限与证据强度
该证据仅限于概念验证和小样本量的试点测试,且数据为摘要级别,表明证据强度为中等,需要更大规模、对照临床评估以确认性能和普适性。

Finger-actuated microfluidic device for point-of-care circulating cell-free nucleic acid extraction.

Authors: Ghulam Musaddaq, Labibah Ehsan, Yano Yoga, Joshua Castillo, Linda Marriott, Nicholas R. Leslie, Deborah A. Williamson, Thomas Tran, Chuan Kok Lim, Maïwenn Kersaudy-Kerhoas
Original abstract (English)
Timely and accurate nucleic acid extraction is a critical bottleneck in point-of-care (POC) molecular diagnostics, particularly for low-abundance cell-free nucleic acids (DNA, RNA, miRNA) in plasma. We present a fully finger-actuated microfluidic device for power-free, pipette-free, nucleic acid extraction. The prototype device integrates finger-actuated valves, pump and blister reagent storage, enabling a complete, intuitive, "sample-to-eluate" workflow, including lysis, binding, washing and elution via manual actuation in approximately 35 minutes. The device operation was benchmarked against a commercial magnetic bead-based manual benchtop and an automated robotic workflow. In DNA-spiked aqueous samples (5-20 ng mL -1 ), the device demonstrated recoveries of 84.6%, against 90.7% on the bench. Optimisation of magnetic bead volume identified 50 μL as the threshold for maximal recovery while minimising reagent consumption. Recovery from human pooled plasma samples were found to be on average 18, 62, and 552% higher than that obtained with the control bench extraction on initial sample volumes of 100, 75 and 50 μL, respectively. Coefficient of variation (CV%) for all techniques remained within reported laboratory ranges (20-80%). The device's clinical utility was piloted using patient samples positive for cytomegalovirus (CMV), monkeypox virus (MPXV) or chikungunya (CHIKV). The device achieved successful qPCR amplification for all targets, with viral DNA Cq values within as little as 0.3 cycles of the standard protocol. This instrument-free, manual circulating nucleic acid extraction tool could help bridge the gap between lateral flow based nucleic acid amplification technologies and real-world decentralised diagnostics, offering a scalable solution for infectious disease management in resource-limited settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the critical need for rapid, accessible nucleic acid extraction at the point of care to enable decentralised molecular diagnostics, particularly for low-abundance circulating cell-free nucleic acids in clinical samples.
Design and population
The authors developed and evaluated a finger-actuated microfluidic device designed for power-free, pipette-free nucleic acid extraction with a manual, instrument-free workflow.
Core methods
The device integrates finger-actuated valves, a pump, and blister-based reagent storage to perform lysis, binding, washing, and elution in approximately 35 minutes, and was benchmarked against commercial magnetic bead-based manual and automated robotic workflows.
Main results
In DNA-spiked aqueous samples (5–20 ng mL−1), the device achieved 84.6% recovery compared to 90.7% for the bench control, while in human pooled plasma, recoveries were 18%, 62%, and 552% higher than the bench extraction at initial volumes of 100, 75, and 50 μL, respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the device as enabling a scalable, decentralised solution for nucleic acid extraction that maintains performance close to standard protocols, with clinical utility demonstrated by successful qPCR amplification from patient samples positive for cytomegalovirus, monkeypox virus, or chikungunya.
Scientific and public-health significance
The device could bridge the gap between lateral flow nucleic acid amplification technologies and decentralised diagnostics, supporting infectious disease management in resource-limited settings where infrastructure and power are constrained.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to proof-of-concept and pilot testing with small sample sizes and abstract-level data, indicating moderate strength and the need for larger, controlled clinical evaluations to confirm performance and generalisability.

利用复合收集系统与优化RPA–CRISPR/Cas12a的高灵敏度病毒生物气溶胶分子检测

High-Sensitivity Molecular Detection of Viral Bioaerosols Using a Composite Collection System and Optimized RPA–CRISPR/Cas12a
作者： Hai Fu, Zhigang Wang, Jianxin Guo, Tengfei Wang, Xiaoyi Li, Yimin Su, Zhi Chen, Huifeng Yang, Zhongjian Xie, Defa Li
摘要中文翻译摘要 高致病性气溶胶微生物对公共卫生构成显著威胁,尤其是其微小粒径可支持长时间悬浮并实现远距离传播。本研究提出了一种集成平台,该平台结合了复合生物气溶胶收集系统与三级富集机制,并采用重组酶聚合酶扩增–CRISPR/Cas12a(RPA–CRISPR/Cas12a)检测。该平台集成了基于模型的恒温控制、叠层光学滤光片及多通道荧光成像。温度控制模型准确描述了反应模块的热响应,而光学与成像子系统则支持稳定的荧光读出。使用气溶胶化猴痘病毒(MPXV)假病毒作为受控测试靶标,在测试条件下,该平台可检测到收集液中低至10拷贝/μL的靶标核酸。这些结果证明了在紧凑原型中集成气溶胶富集与等温分子检测的可行性。主要贡献在于生物气溶胶收集与富集、分子检测中生化反应的热控制以及分子检测精密荧光检测技术的创新与工程集成。在分子检测方法方面,生化反应参数通过实验优化而非引入新的生化反应机制。在做出诊断或现场使用声明前,仍需使用真实临床或环境病毒气溶胶进行验证。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在应对高致病性气溶胶微生物(尤其是粒径极小、可长时间悬浮并远距离传播的病原体)所构成的公共卫生威胁。
设计与对象
本研究提出了一种集成平台,该平台结合了复合生物气溶胶收集系统与三级富集机制,并采用RPA–CRISPR/Cas12a检测。研究使用气溶胶化猴痘病毒(MPXV)假病毒作为受控测试靶标进行验证。
核心方法
该平台集成了基于模型的恒温控制、叠层光学滤光片及多通道荧光成像。生化反应参数通过实验优化而非引入新的生化反应机制。
主要结果
在测试条件下,该平台可检测到收集液中低至10拷贝/μL的靶标核酸。
解释与创新
研究结果支持在紧凑原型中集成气溶胶富集与等温分子检测的可行性,并凸显该平台在高灵敏度、高效病毒生物气溶胶检测方面的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,该平台有望提升对包括猴痘病毒在内的气溶胶传播病原体的检测能力,并增强公共卫生环境中的监测与诊断能力。
局限与证据强度
研究指出,在做出诊断或现场使用声明前,仍需使用真实临床或环境病毒气溶胶进行验证,表明证据强度为中等。

High-Sensitivity Molecular Detection of Viral Bioaerosols Using a Composite Collection System and Optimized RPA–CRISPR/Cas12a

Authors: Hai Fu, Zhigang Wang, Jianxin Guo, Tengfei Wang, Xiaoyi Li, Yimin Su, Zhi Chen, Huifeng Yang, Zhongjian Xie, Defa Li
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Highly pathogenic airborne microorganisms pose a significant public health threat, particularly because their small particle size can support prolonged suspension and long-range transport. Here, we present an integrated platform that combines a composite bioaerosol collection system with a three-stage enrichment mechanism and recombinase polymerase amplification–CRISPR/Cas12a (RPA–CRISPR/Cas12a) detection. The platform incorporates model-based constant-temperature control, stacked optical filters, and multichannel fluorescence imaging. The temperature-control model accurately described the thermal response of the reaction module, while the optical and imaging subsystems supported stable fluorescence readout. Using aerosolized monkeypox virus (MPXV) pseudovirus as a controlled test target, the platform detected target nucleic acid in collection liquid down to 10 copies/μL under the tested conditions. These results support the feasibility of integrating aerosol enrichment with isothermal molecular detection in a compact prototype. The main contributions lie in innovations and the engineering integration of bioaerosol collection and enrichment, thermal control of biochemical reactions in molecular detection, and precision fluorescence detection techniques for molecular detection. Regarding molecular detection methods, the biochemical reaction parameters were experimentally optimized rather than introducing new biochemical reaction mechanisms. Validation with authentic clinical or environmental viral aerosols remains necessary before diagnostic or field-use claims can be made.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the public health threat posed by highly pathogenic airborne microorganisms, particularly those with small particle sizes that can remain suspended for prolonged periods and travel long distances.
Design and population
The study presented an integrated platform combining a composite bioaerosol collection system with a three-stage enrichment mechanism and RPA–CRISPR/Cas12a detection. The platform was validated using aerosolized monkeypox virus (MPXV) pseudovirus as a controlled test target.
Core methods
The platform incorporated model-based constant-temperature control, stacked optical filters, and multichannel fluorescence imaging. The biochemical reaction parameters were experimentally optimized rather than introducing new biochemical reaction mechanisms.
Main results
The platform detected target nucleic acid in collection liquid down to 10 copies/μL under the tested conditions.
Interpretation and novelty
The results support the feasibility of integrating aerosol enrichment with isothermal molecular detection in a compact prototype, highlighting the platform's potential for sensitive and efficient viral bioaerosol detection.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that this platform could improve the detection of airborne pathogens like the mpox virus, enhancing surveillance and diagnostic capabilities in public health settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study notes that validation with authentic clinical or environmental viral aerosols remains necessary before diagnostic or field-use claims can be made, indicating a moderate level of evidence strength.

56 The Prevalence of Uropathogens and Antimicrobial Susceptibility Profiles Encountered in Western India.

56 The Prevalence of Uropathogens and Antimicrobial Susceptibility Profiles Encountered in Western India.
作者： Cardenas E
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在开发一款工具,供感染预防团队按风险等级对疑似麻疹病例进行分层,从而在等待检测结果期间支持决定优先或延迟主动干预。
设计与对象
研究人群包括在实施期间接受麻疹检测的患者,并通过19名额外疑似患者进一步完善风险矩阵。
核心方法
研究构建了一份二维风险矩阵,整合流行病学和临床风险因素,每个因素基于“是”或“否”问题进行数值评分。两类评分之和用于分配最终风险等级。采用描述性统计总结风险类别和结局的分布,并使用Firth logistic回归评估临床或流行病学风险评分与麻疹诊断的关联。
主要结果
该矩阵应用于56名麻疹检测患者,其中10.7%(n=6)被归类为高风险,32.1%(n=18)为中等风险,57.1%(n=32)为低风险。在56名患者中,有4名麻疹检测结果为阳性,其中3名被归类为高风险,1名为中等风险。流行病学风险每增加一个单位,麻疹诊断阳性的几率增加2倍(优势比=2.0;95%置信区间,1.3–3.6)。低风险类别的阴性预测值为1,表明所有低风险患者检测结果均为阴性。
解释与创新
研究结果支持使用该风险矩阵指导感染预防团队基于风险等级优先采取行动,该工具通过统计分析验证,并在不同风险类别中展现出实际应用价值。
科研与公卫意义
该工具可为感染预防团队提供快速、循证的行动依据,尤其在麻疹病例管理和降低低风险人群不必要检测方面具有指导意义。
局限与证据强度
本研究受限于单一队列研究的依赖,以及风险分类可能存在的偏倚。证据强度为中等,因该工具虽通过统计分析验证,但未在更广泛人群中测试。

Original abstract (English)
Infection preventionists (IPs) often balance proactive contact tracing with waiting for a positive test result. Objective: To develop a tool that enables IPs to stratify suspect measles cases by risk level, thereby supporting the decision to prioritize or defer active intervention while awaiting test results. Methods: In spring 2025, we developed a preliminary matrix and used 19 additional suspect patients to further refine the scoring of the tool. The study population included patients tested for measles during the implementation period. Results: Since implementa- tion, the matrix was applied to 56 patients tested for measles. Conclusion: The Measles Exposure Response Risk Matrix effectively stratified cases and supported IP prioritization efforts. Methods: We conducted a longi- tudinal, multi-site investigation of ARG occurrence along the Cuautla River, an urban watershed in Morelos, Mexico, serving the resource-lim- ited city of Cuautla. Results: ARGs were widely detected across the watershed, with particularly high abundances of tetW, CMY, TEM, and multiple Gram-negative carbapenemase genes, including KPC, OXA-48, and VIM. Conclusions: Our findings demonstrate extensive resistome contamination within an urban river system, encom- passing both environmental and clinically important ARGs. Charles Huskins2 1Mayo Clinic Health System- Mankato and 2Mayo Clinic Background: A measles case requires immediate action to identify and administer prophylaxis to exposed individuals in healthcare settings. In August 2025, we deployed a finalized tool. The two-dimensional risk matrix...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a tool for infection prevention teams to stratify suspect measles cases by risk level, supporting decisions on prioritizing or deferring active intervention while awaiting test results.
Design and population
The study population included patients tested for measles during the implementation period, with a focus on refining the risk matrix using 19 additional suspect patients.
Core methods
A two-dimensional risk matrix was developed incorporating both epidemiological and clinical risk factors, each scored numerically based on yes or no questions. The sum of both categories was used to assign a final risk level. Descriptive statistics were used to summarize the distribution of risk categories and outcomes, and Firth’s logistic regression was used to evaluate the association between clinical or epidemiological risk score and measles diagnosis.
Main results
The matrix was applied to 56 patients tested for measles, with 10.7% (n=6) categorized as high risk, 32.1% (n=18) as medium, and 57.1% (n=32) as low. Four of the 56 patients were positive for measles, with 3 categorized as high-risk and 1 as medium risk. Each one-unit increase in epidemiological risk was associated with a 2-fold increase in the odds of a positive measles diagnosis (odds ratio = 2.0; 95% CI, 1.3–3.6). The low-risk category had an NPV of 1, indicating that all low-risk patients tested negative.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings support the use of the risk matrix to guide infection prevention teams in prioritizing actions based on risk levels, with the tool validated by statistical analysis and demonstrating practical application across different risk categories.
Scientific and public-health significance
The tool can inform rapid, evidence-based actions by infection prevention teams, particularly in managing measles cases and reducing unnecessary testing in low-risk groups.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on a single cohort and the potential for bias in risk categorization. The evidence is moderate, as the tool was validated using statistical analysis but not tested in a broader population.

59 Clear After a Year? Insights from a Pediatric Approach to Contact Precaution Discontinuation for Resistant Gram-Negatives Organisms

59 Clear After a Year? Insights from a Pediatric Approach to Contact Precaution Discontinuation for Resistant Gram-Negatives Organisms
作者： Piccolo A, Kaplan-Lewis E, Gala K, Casey E, Cohen G, Rasmussen S, Wallach A, Mgbako O, Chan J
摘要中文翻译这些结果凸显了将环境水监测纳入抗菌药物耐药性(AMR)监测框架的必要性,并支持一种“一健康”方法,将废水管理、抗菌药物合理使用与公共卫生保护相结合,特别是在资源有限的环境中。本研究旨在描述符合条件的H+H患者群体中猴痘疫苗接种模式。方法:H+H于2022年5月开始使用JYNNEOS疫苗进行疫苗接种,并通过电子健康记录(EHR)提醒鼓励符合条件人群接种第二剂疫苗,包括:(1) HIV感染者且过去一年内患有性传播感染者;或(2) 已接种至少一剂猴痘疫苗且已到第二剂接种时间的患者;或(3) EHR中性取向登记为男同性恋、双性恋、女同性恋、其他或选择不披露者;或(4) EHR中性别认同登记为跨性别女性、跨性别男性、性别非二元/非顺性别、其他或选择不披露者。结果:图1总结了H+H猴痘疫苗接种时间线上的关键事件。结论:在符合条件的H+H患者中,我们发现猴痘疫苗接种率在不同种族/民族、社区及邮政编码社会脆弱性指数(SVI)方面存在差异。《抗菌药物合理使用与医疗保健》2026;6(Suppl. S1):s42–s43 doi:10.1017/ash.2026.10489 报告类型:口头报告 学科类别:猴痘 文摘编号:59 一年后是否“清零”?从儿科角度对耐药革兰阴性菌接触预防措施终止的洞察 Anthony Lo Piccolo1,Emma Kaplan-Lewis2,Kruti Gala3,Eunice Casey3,Gabriel Cohen4,Stephanie Rasmussen5,Andrew Wallach4,Ofole Mgbako5 及 Justin Chan5 1纽约大学朗格尼医疗中心;2纽约市卫生与医院管理局;3纽约市卫生+医院;4纽约市卫生+医院/贝尔维尤医院;5纽约大学格罗斯曼医学院
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在描述符合条件的H+H患者群体中猴痘疫苗接种模式,并凸显不同种族/民族、社区及邮政编码社会脆弱性指数(SVI)在猴痘疫苗接种率方面的差异。
设计与对象
本研究由纽约市卫生+医院(H+H)于2022年5月至2025年4月开展,涵盖112,680名符合条件的H+H患者,包括HIV感染者、需接种第二剂猴痘疫苗的患者,以及具有特定性取向或性别认同的患者。
核心方法
H+H通过电子健康记录(EHR)提醒鼓励符合条件患者接种疫苗,并从纽约市全市免疫登记系统提取数据。研究计算了2022年CDC社会脆弱性指数(SVI)与各邮政编码猴痘疫苗接种率之间的皮尔逊相关系数。
主要结果
截至2025年4月,25.7%的符合条件H+H患者至少接种了一剂猴痘疫苗,其中HIV感染者的接种率为51.5%。白人患者的猴痘疫苗接种比例最高(31.1%),而西班牙裔/拉丁裔患者最低(22.0%)。猴痘疫苗接种率与SVI呈负相关(r= -0.0529;p<0.01)。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为猴痘疫苗接种率在种族/民族、社区及SVI方面存在差异的证据,并指出需通过质量改进措施优化猴痘疫苗接种公平性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可为改善猴痘疫苗接种公平性及解决不同SVI地区猴痘疫苗接种率差异的策略提供参考,特别是在资源有限且社会脆弱性高的环境中。
局限与证据强度
研究可能存在对符合条件个体的误分类,该分类依赖于EHR中结构化社会史与性别认同数据的准确记录。证据强度为中等,因其基于观察性数据与相关性分析。

Insights from a Pediatric Approach to Contact Precaution Discontinuation for Resistant Gram-Negatives Organisms Anthony Lo Piccolo1, Emma Kaplan-Lewis2, Kruti Gala3, Eunice Casey3, Gabriel Cohen4, Stephanie Rasmussen5, Andrew Wallach4, Ofole Mgbako5 and Justin Chan5 1NYU Langone Health; 2NYC Health and Hospitals; 3NYC Health + Hospitals; 4NYC Health + Hospitals/Bellevue and 5NYU Grossman School...

Question and background
This study aimed to describe patterns of mpox vaccination amongst eligible H+H patient groups, highlighting disparities in vaccination rates by race/ethnicity, neighborhood, and the zip code Social Vulnerability Index (SVI).
Design and population
The study was conducted by NYC Health + Hospitals (H+H) from May 2022 to April 2025, focusing on 112,680 eligible H+H patients, including those with HIV, those due for a second mpox vaccine, and those with specific sexual orientations or gender identities.
Core methods
H+H used electronic health record (EHR) reminders to encourage vaccination among eligible patients, and data were extracted from the NYC Citywide Immunization Registry. A Pearson correlation coefficient was calculated between the 2022 CDC Social Vulnerability Index (SVI) and vaccination rates by zip code.
Main results
As of April 2025, 25.7% of eligible H+H patients received at least one mpox dose, with 51.5% of patients with HIV vaccinated. White patients had the highest vaccination proportion (31.1%), and Hispanic/Latinx patients had the lowest (22.0%). Vaccination rates were negatively correlated with SVI (r= -0.0529; p<0.01).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as evidence of disparities in mpox vaccination rates by race/ethnicity, neighborhood, and SVI, identifying areas for quality improvement to optimize vaccine equity.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings can inform strategies to improve vaccine equity and address disparities in mpox vaccination rates, particularly in resource-limited settings with high social vulnerability.
Limitations and evidence strength
A limitation may be misclassification of eligible individuals, which relies on structured social history and gender identity data accurately documented in the EHR. The evidence is moderate, as it is based on observational data and correlation analysis.

2025财年医院获得性疾病减少计划中医院处罚的60个预测因子

60 Predictors of Hospital Penalties in the Hospital-Acquired Condition Reduction Program, Fiscal Year 2025
作者： Lee J, Kang J
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在描述住院患者抗生素相关过敏反应(包括严重事件)的特征及特点,并确定因报告过敏而不适当调整抗生素方案的比例。
设计与对象
我们对2022–2024年在韩国延世大学医院(大韩民国)就诊且年龄≤18岁、接受口服或静脉抗菌药物治疗并在电子健康记录中记录为“过敏”的抗菌药物相关不良药物反应(ADR)患者进行了回顾性研究。共记录了215例抗菌药物相关ADR(0.92%),其中包括161例过敏反应事件,涉及122名患者(0.7%),与143份抗菌药物处方相关。
核心方法
我们应用了多变量混合效应逻辑回归模型(含随机设施截距)评估VHA设施间抗生素处方变异,调整了患者人口统计学特征、白细胞计数及设施复杂性水平。通过自助重抽样(2,000次迭代)估计了中位数比值比(MOR)的95%置信区间。
主要结果
在31,164例住院病例中,17,614例(56.0%)患者接受了抗生素治疗。6名患者发生了过敏性休克(占反应的5.0%;占抗生素处方的0.002%)。设施间抗生素处方变异的MOR为1.70(95%自助重抽样置信区间:1.00–2.22),提示两名在其他方面相同的患者在随机选择的设施接受治疗时,获得抗生素处方的几率中位数相差70%。
解释与创新
住院儿童的真实抗生素过敏较为罕见,但近三分之一的报告过敏导致了抗生素使用不当或不合理。
科研与公卫意义
同时监测耐药模式与抗菌药物使用模式对于指导经验性处方实践与管理策略至关重要。
局限与证据强度
设施特异性调整后OR值范围为0.26(95% CI:0.14–0.48)至1.50(95% CI:0.60–3.79),表明VHA系统内处方实践存在异质性。

Authors: Lee J, Kang J

View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to characterize the incidence and features of inpatient antibiotic-associated allergic reactions, including serious events, and to determine the proportion of antibiotic regimens that were inappropriately modified in response to reported allergies.
Design and population
We conducted a retrospective study of patients ≤18 years admitted to Severance Children’s Hospital (Republic of Korea) during 2022–2024 who received oral or intravenous antibacterials and had an antibiotic-related adverse drug reaction (ADR) documented as "allergy" in the electronic health record. A total of 215 antibiotic-related ADRs (0.92%) were documented, including 161 allergic-reaction events in 122 patients (0.7%) associated with 143 antibiotic prescriptions.
Core methods
We applied a multivariable mixed-effects logistic regression model with random facility intercepts to assess variation in antibiotic prescribing across VHA facilities, adjusting for patient demographics, WBC count, and facility complexity level. Adjusted odds ratios (ORs) and 95% confidence intervals (CIs) were estimated to assess the associations between patient-level variables and antibiotic prescription. Bootstrap resampling (2,000 iterations) was used to estimate 95% CIs for the MOR.
Main results
Among 31,164 admissions, 17,614 (56.0%) patients received antibiotics. Anaphylaxis occurred in 6 patients (5.0% of reactions; 0.002% of antibiotic prescriptions). The MOR for the facility-level variation in antibiotic prescribing was 1.70 (95% bootstrap CI: 1.00-2.22), suggesting a median 70% difference in the odds of receiving antibiotics between two otherwise identical patients treated at randomly selected facilities.
Interpretation and novelty
True antibiotic allergy in hospitalized children is uncommon, yet nearly one third of reported allergies led to suboptimal or inappropriate antibiotic use.
Scientific and public-health significance
Surveillance of both resistance patterns and antimicrobial utilization is essential to inform empiric prescribing practices and stewardship strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
Facility-specific adjusted ORs ranged from 0.26 (95% CI: 0.14-0.48) to 1.50 (95% CI: 0.60-3.79), indicating heterogeneity in prescribing practices across the VHA system.

VA 社区生活中心疫苗接种居民中 196 例突破性 COVID-19 感染

196 Breakthrough COVID-19 Infections Among Vaccinated Residents of VA Community Living Centers
作者： Smith B
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估生成型人工智能(GenAI)在评估医院获得性菌血症和真菌血症(HOB)事件的归因和可预防性方面的应用,以应对医疗环境中快速且准确识别来源和可预防性的需求。
设计与对象
回顾性队列研究研究在11家美国退伍军人事务管理局(VA)医院进行,纳入了入院第3天之后采集的阳性血培养样本(入院日为第1天)。该研究共纳入52例IP-AI事件,其中21例为IP-alone的子集。
核心方法
GenAI(OpenAI GPT-4o)被提示提供最可能的HOB来源,并根据标准化提示和临床数据判断其可预防性。IP专家利用GenAI的输出结果,对HOB的来源和可预防性进行GenAI辅助判断,同时对随机选取的子集进行了单独的人类评审,以比较评审所需时间。
主要结果
GenAI最常将HOB归因于胃肠道(GI)来源(26.9%),其次是泌尿生殖系统(GU)(23%)、中心静脉导管相关血流感染(CLABSI)(15%)以及皮肤和软组织感染(SSTI)(14%)。在IP-AI对HOB来源的判断中,有67%(35/52)的案例与GenAI一致,当HOB归因于骨关节感染、胃肠道来源、皮肤和软组织感染以及泌尿生殖系统来源时,一致性最高。完成IP-AI评审的中位时间是11.5分钟(IQR 6 – 20.5),而IP-alone的中位时间是25分钟(IQR 13 – 33)。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,IP专家通常同意GenAI对HOB来源的判断,但对其可预防性评估存在分歧,突显了GenAI在加速HOB来源识别方面的潜力,同时强调了在可预防性判断中需要人类监督的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果表明,将GenAI整合到HOB 监测中可能有助于加快来源识别,从而潜在地改善医疗环境中的感染预防策略,尽管可预防性评估仍需要专家验证。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于依赖单一AI模型和相对较小的样本量,这可能影响研究结果的普遍适用性。由于研究设计为回顾性,且AI解释可能存在偏倚,因此证据强度中等。

196 Breakthrough COVID-19 Infections Among Vaccinated Residents of VA Community Living Centers

Authors: Smith B
Original abstract (English)
In this study, we analyze microbiological testing data from more than 200,000 healthcare-associated water system samples collected since September 2019, comparing pathogen positivity across ice machines, gen- eral potable water, and cooling towers. Results demonstrate substantial variability in pathogen positivity by system type, with NTM showing mark- edly higher positivity in ice machines and potable water systems (relative to traditional indicator organisms). Presentation Type: Poster Presentation Subject Category: Surveillance Abstract ID: 196 Breakthrough COVID-19 Infections Among Vaccinated Residents of VA Community Living Centers Brandon Smith1 EST Healthcare water management programs (WMPs) have historically emphasized the control of Legionella pneumophila, largely due to its estab- lished role in Legionnaires’ disease outbreaks. However, healthcare water systems harbor a wide range of opportunistic premise plumbing pathogens (OPPPs), including nontuberculous mycobacteria (NTM), P...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the use of generative artificial intelligence (GenAI) in assessing the attribution and preventability of hospital-onset bacteremia and fungemia (HOB) events, addressing the need for efficient and accurate methods to identify sources and preventability in healthcare settings.
Design and population
A retrospective cohort study was conducted at 11 US Veterans Affairs (VA) hospitals, including a random sample of positive blood cultures collected after day 3 of admission where admission date is day 1. The study included 52 HOB events for IP-AI and a subset of 21 for IP-alone.
Core methods
GenAI (OpenAI GPT-4o) was prompted to provide the most likely source and adjudicate the preventability of HOB using standardized prompts and clinical data. IP experts used GenAI output to provide GenAI-assisted determinations of source and preventability, and a separate human-only review by a different IP expert was performed on a random subset to compare time required for review.
Main results
GenAI most commonly attributed HOB to gastrointestinal (GI) sources (26.9%), followed by genitourinary (GU) (23%), central line (CLABSI) (15%), and skin and soft tissue infections (SSTI) (14%). IP-AI determination of source agreed with GenAI in 67% (35/52) of cases, with most agreement when HOB was attributed to bone and joint infections, GI sources, SSTI, and GU sources. The median time to complete an IP-AI review was 11.5 (IQR 6 – 20.5) minutes compared to a median of 25 (IQR 13 – 33) minutes for IP-alone.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings indicate that IP experts often agree with GenAI for the source of HOB but disagree with its preventability assessment, highlighting the potential of GenAI to expedite HOB source identification while emphasizing the need for human oversight in preventability judgments.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results suggest that integrating GenAI into HOB surveillance could enhance the speed of source identification, potentially improving infection prevention strategies in healthcare settings, although preventability assessments require expert validation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on a single AI model and a relatively small sample size, which may affect the generalizability of findings. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the retrospective design and potential biases in AI interpretation.
